- **uncertainty**: Use when reasoning about the Heisenberg uncertainty floor — two conjugate quantities (position⊗momentum, energy⊗time) can never both be sharp, their spreads obey Δa·Δb ≥ ħ/2. It is the wave nature of the state, not a measurement defect. In erpax it is a real design limit: the irreducible slack that can never reach zero, and the natural linewidth that broadens every spectrum line (a leap living only Δt has ΔE ≥ ħ/2Δt). - **understand-check**: Before doing expensive or ambiguous work, echoes back the task and assumptions in a few lines so the user can confirm or correct BEFORE the effort is spent. - **understand-colbert_late_interaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_late_interaction` **DEFINITION:** Core architectural principle deferring interaction between query and document token representations until scoring time, enabling efficient per-token similarity computation without full cross-encoding of query-document pairs. - **understand-colbert_maxsim_operator**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_maxsim_operator` **DEFINITION:** Maximum similarity operator that for each query token finds the highest-scoring document token via cosine similarity, then aggregates across query tokens to produce document relevance score. - **understand-colbert_token_embedding**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_token_embedding` **DEFINITION:** Dense contextualized vector representation produced per token by an encoder model, preserving token identity and positional context for late interaction scoring. - **understand-cp_purge_trigger**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_purge_trigger : deep_message(6), enterprise_integration_patterns(3)` **DEFINITION:** A condition that initiates a channel purge cycle; may be time-based, count-based, or event-based such as threshold_exceeded. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_enterprise_integration_patterns/understand-cp_purge_trigger) · ★1)
- **understand-cp_stale_message**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_stale_message` **DEFINITION:** A message that has exceeded its relevance window or idle duration and no longer carries actionable data for downstream consumers. - **understand-cp_threshold_condition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_threshold_condition : deep_message(6), enterprise_integration_patterns(3)` **DEFINITION:** A predicate over channel_state_snapshot values that evaluates to true when purge_trigger should fire, such as depth_overflow or stale_age_exceeded. - **understand-design**: Use when the user has a design prototype (from Stitch, Figma, or any tool) and wants AI to analyze it. Works standalone for daily development or in pipeline mode with a design doc. Handles images, HTML/CSS, or both. Not when: user wants the prototype audited AGAINST an existing implementation (use apple-dev:design-parity-build — this skill analyzes the design itself, with no codebase comparison); user wants to GENERATE a design prompt FOR a tool (use /generate-design-prompt — inverse direction: project context → prompt text). - **understand-distributed_transaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.distributed_transaction` **DEFINITION:** A transaction that spans multiple independent nodes or services, requiring coordination across participants to ensure atomicity, typically via two-phase commit, three-phase commit, or compensating transaction patterns. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-distributed_transaction) · ★1)
- **understand-djt_bounding_range**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_bounding_range` **DEFINITION:** The closed interval [min_delay, previous_delay times decorrelation_factor] from which the uniform random sample is drawn to compute the next current_delay; bounds the worst-case wait while allowing history-dependent growth. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_bounding_range) · ★1)
- **understand-djt_random_uniform_sample**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_random_uniform_sample` **DEFINITION:** The uniform random draw within the bounding range that produces the new current_delay value; the randomness decorrelates this thread's retry timing from concurrent threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_random_uniform_sample) · ★1)
- **understand-djt_wave_attenuation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_wave_attenuation` **DEFINITION:** The emergent property that decorrelated jitter reduces the probability of simultaneous retries among competing threads; achieved through per-thread history-dependent ranges that diverge over time. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-djt_wave_attenuation) · ★1)

- **understand-dps_002**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_002` **DEFINITION:** schema_isolation means each service's database exposes only the data and operations relevant to its bounded context, hiding internal structures from other services. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-dps_002) · ★1)
- **understand-dps_003**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_003 : software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(4)` **DEFINITION:** instance_isolation means each service runs its own database server process or cluster, with separate credentials, ports, and storage, preventing any service from accessing another's data store directly. - **understand-dps_006**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_microservices_archit.dps_006 : software_architecture_patterns_and_styles(15), deep_event_driven_archite(9)` **DEFINITION:** data_duplication is the consequence of database_per_service where the same logical entity may be materialized in multiple service databases, kept synchronized through application-level events. - **understand-lactobacillus_esterase_activity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_fermentation.lactobacillus_esterase_activity : deep_lactobacillus(6), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** Enzymatic capability of lactobacillus strains to catalyze esterification reactions between organic_acids and ethanol during sourdough fermentation ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lactobacillus_esterase_activity) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_flavor_output_signature**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_flavor_output_signature : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(7), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The characteristic ratio of fruity_ester_notes and floral_ester_notes (from lactate esters) versus sharp vinegar-like nuances (from acetate esters) in the final sourdough aroma profile resulting from lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_flavor_output_signature) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_lactate_bias**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_lactate_bias : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(7), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The enzymatic state wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity exhibits lower Km and higher turnover for lactic_acid over acetic_acid, directing condensation flux through le_lactate_ester_pathway to yield lactate ester dominance. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_lactate_bias) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(8), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The proportional distribution of esterification catalysis between le_lactate_ester_pathway and le_acetate_ester_pathway as determined by the relative kinetic favorability of each acyl donor substrate. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_ph_modulation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_ph_modulation : sourdough_baking(3), deep_fermentation(3), deep_lactobacillus(1)` **DEFINITION:** The influence of dough pH on the ionization state of organic_acid substrates, affecting their availability as le_organic_acid_substrate and thus modulating lactobacillus_esterase_activity acyl donor preference. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_ph_modulation) · ★1)
- **understand-lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(8), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The instantaneous comparison of le_kinetic_properties for esterification versus hydrolysis determining whether net accumulation of fc_estolide_compounds occurs at any fermentation moment. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio) · ★1)
- **understand-lch_hydrolysis_reaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lch_hydrolysis_reaction : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(9), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The reverse chemical reaction wherein water molecule attacks the ester bond of fc_estolide_compounds, cleaving them back into le_ethanol_substrate and le_organic_acid_substrate, releasing the water_byproduct consumed in condensation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lch_hydrolysis_reaction) · ★1)
- **understand-le_acetate_ester_pathway**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_acetate_ester_pathway : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The specific esterification route wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity catalyzes the reaction of acetic_acid with ethanol to form acetate esters contributing to sharp, vinegar-like aromatic nuances in sourdough headspace. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_acetate_ester_pathway) · ★1)
- **understand-le_activity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_activity : deep_fermentation(29), deep_le_activity(13), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** Enzymatic capability of lactobacillus strains to catalyze esterification reactions between organic_acids and ethanol during sourdough fermentation, producing ester compounds that contribute to flavor_complexity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_activity) · ★1)
- **understand-le_acyl_donor_preference**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_acyl_donor_preference : deep_fermentation(29), deep_le_activity(9), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The substrate specificity of lactobacillus_esterase_activity determining whether lactic_acid or acetic_acid serves as the primary acyl donor, influencing the resulting ester profile. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_acyl_donor_preference) · ★1)
- **understand-le_estolide_formation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_estolide_formation : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5), deep_le_activity(4)` **DEFINITION:** The biochemical process by which lactobacillus_esterase_activity joins organic_acid acyl groups with ethanol alcohol groups through condensation, yielding fc_estolide_compounds and water as products. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_estolide_formation) · ★1)
- **understand-le_lactate_ester_pathway**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_lactate_ester_pathway : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The specific esterification route wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity catalyzes the reaction of lactic_acid (derived from pyruvate reduction) with ethanol to form lactate esters contributing to fruity_ester_notes and floral_ester_notes. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_lactate_ester_pathway) · ★1)
- **understand-le_organic_acid_substrate**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_organic_acid_substrate : sourdough_baking(3)` **DEFINITION:** Carbon-chain acid substrates (lactic_acid and acetic_acid) from lactobacillus metabolism that serve as acyl donors in esterification reactions catalyzed by lactobacillus_esterase_activity. - **understand-le_substrates**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_substrates : sourdough_baking(3), deep_fermentation(2)` **DEFINITION:** The molecular reactants consumed during lactobacillus_esterase_activity: organic_acids and ethanol that undergo esterification catalysis. - **understand-maximum_position**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_context_window.maximum_position` **DEFINITION:** The highest valid integer position index representable by the position_embedding scheme within context_length; equal to max_model_tokens minus one for zero-indexed sequences. - **understand-mcs_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.mcs_lock` **DEFINITION:** Queued lock where each waiting thread spins on a locally-owned node linked into a queue; cache-friendly with only O(1) bus traffic per lock acquisition; named after Mellor-Crummey and Scott. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock) · ★1)
- **understand-mcs_lock_enqueue**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_enqueue` **DEFINITION:** Operation appending a thread-local qnode to the queue tail; atomically CASes qnode into tail; on CAS failure, completes the predecessor's link and retries; ensures FIFO acquisition order. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_enqueue) · ★1)
- **understand-mcs_lock_qnode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_qnode` **DEFINITION:** Thread-local queue node containing locked flag and successor pointer; must be cache-aligned and allocated per-thread to enable local spinning without shared contention. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_qnode) · ★1)
- **understand-mcs_lock_release**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_release` **DEFINITION:** Operation by which a thread releases the lock; sets own qnode.locked to false to unblock spinning successor; if successor exists in qnode.next, responsibility transfers to successor's own spin; cache-friendly O(1) handoff. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_release) · ★1)
- **understand-memory_entry**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_retrieval_augmented_.memory_entry` **DEFINITION:** Atomic unit of stored information in the memory system, containing content plus metadata ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_retrieval_augmented_generation_architecture_patt/understand-memory_entry) · ★1)
- **understand-memory_order_acquire**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_synchronizes_with.memory_order_acquire : deep_happens_before_relat(5)` **DEFINITION:** Memory ordering barrier ensuring all loads and stores after the barrier in program order cannot be reordered before it, synchronizing with release stores. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_acquire) · ★1)
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.memory_order_relaxed : deep_c11_memory_model(39), deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_indivisibility_prope(9), deep_happens_before_relat(7)` **DEFINITION:** C11/C++11 memory ordering that only guarantees atomicity of the operation with no ordering constraints relative to other operations; allows all reorderings. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed) · ★1)
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_indivisibility_prope(9), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(5)` **DEFINITION:** The indivisibility guarantee that a relaxed atomic operation completes as a single indivisible step; no intermediate state is observable by other threads during ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee) · ★1)
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location` **DEFINITION:** The property that each atomic variable maintains its own independent coherence order but this order is not coordinated with coherence orders of other variables; operations on separate variables are totally unordered with respect to each other. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location) · ★1)

- **understand-rmw_ind_003**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_003 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** modify_phase: the purely computational step wherein the value captured in the read phase is transformed by the operation's specific function (addition, subtraction, bitwise OR, bitwise AND, exclusive OR, maximum, minimum, or conditional replacement); this phase produces the value to be written but d ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-rmw_ind_003) · ★1)

- **understand-rmw_ind_006**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_006 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** modification_order_commitment: the serialization of the RMW operation into the global modification order of the target atomic variable at the instant the write_phase becomes visible; this ordering point determines the value returned by concurrent operations. - **understand-rr_definition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_definition` **DEFINITION:** An isolation_level ensuring that all reads within a transaction see a consistent snapshot of the database as of transaction start; prevents non_repeatable_read by acquiring and holding read locks on accessed rows until transaction end. - **understand-rr_read_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_read_lock` **DEFINITION:** A shared_lock acquired when a row is read under repeatable_read; held until transaction commits or rolls back, preventing any concurrent transaction from acquiring a write_lock on that row. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rr_read_lock) · ★1)
- **understand-rrf_fusion_score**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.rrf_fusion_score` **DEFINITION:** The sum of reciprocal rank values for a given document across all contributing rank lists; the document's position in the fused ranking is determined by descending fusion score. - **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility` **DEFINITION:** The guarantee that all threads observe the effects of a sequentially consistent operation at the same logical point in the global total order; reads observe the most recent write in the total order. - **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A seq_cst read cannot observe a write that is ordered after it in the total order; the happens-before relationship established by seq_cst operations enforces a cutoff beyond which writes are not visible. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility) · ★1)

([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_global_total_order**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_global_total_order : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(3)` **DEFINITION:** A single total order of all sequentially consistent operations that is visible and agreed upon by all threads in the system; the interleaving point where all threads observe the same sequence of operation ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_global_total_order) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity` **DEFINITION:** The property that each sequentially consistent operation appears indivisible and instantaneous to all observers; no intermediate states are visible during the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_synchronization_protocol : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(1)` **DEFINITION:** The protocol by which sequentially consistent operations establish synchronization points between threads; creates a happens-before relationship across thread boundaries. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol) · ★1)
- **understand-sequentially_consistent**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.sequentially_consistent : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_c11_memory_model(16), deep_happens_before_relat(7)` **DEFINITION:** The strongest memory ordering requiring a single total order of all sequentially consistent operations visible to all threads; implied by default for std::atomic operations in C++. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-sequentially_consistent) · ★1)
- **understand-sha1_hash**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.sha1_hash : deep_commit_object(8), deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** 160-bit cryptographic hash computed over object content; serves as object's unique addressable name in git's content-addressable store. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-sha1_hash) · ★1)

- **understand-slip_card**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_knowledge_management.slip_card : personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra(11)` **DEFINITION:** A single note card serving as the atomic unit of a slip-box; the fundamental indivisible element that carries one idea. - **understand-split_brain**: **CALL NUMBER:** `distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols.split_brain : deep_consensus(7), deep_raft(6)` **DEFINITION:** Scenario where network partition causes multiple nodes to believe they are leader; resolved by quorum. - **understand-statement_level_read_consistency**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_isolation_level.statement_level_read_consistency` **DEFINITION:** guarantee within read_committed that each SQL statement sees all changes committed at the moment that statement began execution ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-statement_level_read_consistency) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_log` **DEFINITION:** An immutable append-only sequence of events retained on disk or equivalent durable storage; unlike pubsub_message which is ephemeral, events in a stream_log persist for the configured retention period and can be reread from any offset by any consumer group. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_log) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_offset**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_offset` **DEFINITION:** A monotonically increasing integer position marker assigned to each event within a partition, uniquely identifying the event's place in the partition's sequence and enabling consumers to resume reading from a specific point. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_offset) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_partition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_partition` **DEFINITION:** A distinct, ordered slice of an event stream, identified by a partition key or index, enabling parallel production and consumption; each partition maintains its own offset sequence independently of other partitions. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_partition) · ★1)
- **understand-structural_pattern**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.structural_pattern` **DEFINITION:** A design pattern category focused on composing classes and objects into larger structures, defining relationships between entities to build flexible and efficient hierarchies. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_design_patterns/understand-structural_pattern) · ★1)
- **understand-subtree_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.subtree_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 040000; entry points to a nested tree object representing a directory; the tree entry name contains a forward slash but the slash is not stored in the entry itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-subtree_mode) · ★1)

- **understand-symlink_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.symlink_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 120000; entry points to a blob object containing the target path as raw bytes; the blob content is the symlink destination, not the link data itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-symlink_mode) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.synchronizes_with : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), deep_c11_memory_model(1)` **DEFINITION:** A relation between atomic operations in memory models where a release fence on one thread synchronizes with an acquire fence on another thread, establishing happens-before order across threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with_absence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.synchronizes_with_absence : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A relaxed atomic operation does not synchronize with any other operation; it establishes no happens-before or synchronizes-with relationship with any thread, including the thread that performed the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with_absence) · ★1)

- **understand-task**: Understand a pasted task step by step before implementation. ([djokobozinov/agent-skills](https://github.com/djokobozinov/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/understand-task))
- **understand-tbox**: **CALL NUMBER:** `knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies.tbox` **DEFINITION:** The terminological box — the schema-level component of a knowledge base defining classes, properties, and their relations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies/understand-tbox) · ★1)
- **understand-te_capturing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_capturing` **DEFINITION:** The first phase of the CODE framework where information is quickly captured before it fades from working memory; a frictionless gateway that accepts raw input without requiring immediate structuring. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_capturing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_distilling**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_distilling` **DEFINITION:** The process of extracting essential insights from source material through progressive summarization; a compression operation that retains meaning while reducing volume. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_distilling) · ★1)
- **understand-te_expressing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_expressing` **DEFINITION:** The final phase of the CODE framework where accumulated knowledge becomes tangible output; the conversion operation that transforms internal knowledge structures into external artifacts. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_expressing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_organizing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_organizing` **DEFINITION:** The structuring operation that transforms captured material into a retrievable system; grouping related content by semantic relationship to enable future discovery and connection. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_organizing) · ★1)

- **understand-theory**: Extract mathematical formulation, statistical models, and theoretical foundations from a bioinformatics method paper ([yyw-informatics/kb-skills-bioinformatics](https://github.com/yyw-informatics/kb-skills-bioinformatics/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/understand-theory) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_contention_surge` **DEFINITION:** A transient spike in the rate of lock or resource acquisition attempts triggered by mass wakeup, producing queue buildup and wasted retry cycles. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_retry_coordination` **DEFINITION:** The phenomenon where concurrently woken threads retry a failing operation in near-lockstep, reinforcing the contention surge; mitigated by jitter applied to retry delays. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wait_set**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wait_set` **DEFINITION:** A collection of threads blocked on a single synchronization point awaiting a condition; the structural prerequisite for thundering-herd because coordinated wakeup of this set is the failure mode. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wait_set) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wakeup_event` **DEFINITION:** A single signal or broadcast that causes ALL members of a wait_set to transition from blocked to runnable simultaneously; the atomic cause of the thundering-herd pattern. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_checkpoint_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_checkpoint_record` **DEFINITION:** A special log_record written periodically by a checkpoint_writer; records the volatile database state at a consistent point, bounding the recovery_manager's replay horizon. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_checkpoint_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_flush_to_disk**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_flush_to_disk` **DEFINITION:** The act of transferring the contents of tl_log_buffer to durable storage; triggered by transaction commit, tl_log_buffer overflow, or a periodic checkpoint; O(1) or O(n) bounded by buffer size. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_flush_to_disk) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_log_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_log_record` **DEFINITION:** The fundamental unit of a transaction_log: a single append-only entry recording one atomic change or lifecycle event made by a transaction; identified by an lsn. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_log_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_recovery_manager**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_recovery_manager` **DEFINITION:** The subsystem that, on system restart, reads the transaction_log from the last tl_checkpoint_record forward; replays committed tl_redo_log entries and rolls back uncommitted transactions using tl_undo_log. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_recovery_manager) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_undo_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_undo_log` **DEFINITION:** A structured collection of tl_update_record entries chained by a transaction; traversed in reverse order during rollback to restore the before_image of each modified entity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_undo_log) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_update_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_update_record` **DEFINITION:** A log_record capturing a before_image and after_image of a modified data page or row; used by both redo_recovery (for durability) and undo_recovery (for atomicity rollback). ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_update_record) · ★1)
- **understand-token_boundary_marker**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_context_window.token_boundary_marker` **DEFINITION:** The positional delimiter marking the transition from input context to output generation; tokens before this marker are provided context, tokens after are to be generated. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_llm_memory_and_context_engineering/understand-token_boundary_marker) · ★1)
- **understand-token_endpoint**: **CALL NUMBER:** `oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect.token_endpoint : deep_oauth_2_0(1)` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 endpoint accepting authorization_code or other grants and returning access_token, refresh_token, and optional id_token. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-token_endpoint) · ★1)
- **understand-token_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.token_type` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 parameter indicating how the access_token is to be presented to the resource server, typically Bearer, specifying the authorization scheme to use in requests. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-token_type) · ★1)
- **understand-transaction_state**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.transaction_state : deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The current phase of a transaction lifecycle: active, partially_committed, committed, aborted; drives lock release and recovery behavior. - **understand-tree_entry_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.tree_entry_mode : deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** Octal mode in tree object: 100644 (regular file), 100755 (executable), 040000 (subtree), 120000 (symlink), 160000 (submodule).
